{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"braids","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"braiding","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"braided","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"braided","tags":["past"]},{"form":"brayde","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"breyde","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"broid","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To make a sudden movement with, to jerk."],"tags":["obsolete","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To start into motion."],"tags":["archaic","intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To weave together, intertwine (strands of fibers, ribbons, etc.); to arrange (hair) in braids."],"synonyms":[{"word":"interlace"},{"word":"interthread"},{"word":"twine"},{"word":"betwine"},{"word":"braid"},{"word":"entwist"},{"word":"entwine"},{"word":"interlace"},{"word":"interthread"},{"word":"intertwine"},{"word":"intertwist"},{"word":"interweave"},{"word":"lace"},{"word":"lock"},{"word":"raddle"},{"word":"ruddle"},{"word":"twine"},{"word":"twist"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To mix, or make uniformly soft, by beating, rubbing, or straining, as in preparing food."]},{"glosses":["To reproach; to upbraid."],"tags":["obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/bɹeɪd/"}],"upstream_line":29337,"upstream_offset":194870294,"upstream_record_sha256":"54c8436fda21cfb6a596df209aadc525ce9ebc4effbc350f17a5978a9dc29c9c","word":"braid"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"braids","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"brayde","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"breyde","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"broid","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A sudden movement; a jerk, a wrench."],"tags":["countable","obsolete"]},{"glosses":["A weave of three or more strands of fibers, ribbons, cords or hair often for decoration."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A stranded wire composed of a number of smaller wires twisted together."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A tubular sheath made of braided strands of metal placed around a central cable for shielding against electromagnetic interference."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A caprice or outburst of passion or anger."],"tags":["countable","obsolete","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Given two sets of n points on corresponding positions on two parallel lines, a braid is a unique set of crossings (over or under) between n strands that connect each point on one line to a point on the other line such that all points represent the terminus of one and only one strand and the traversal of any strand from a starting point to an ending point never moves further away from the from the ending point."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["mathematics","sciences","topology"]},{"glosses":["A wicker guard for protecting newly grafted trees."],"tags":["countable","dialectal","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A moment, stound."],"tags":["countable","obsolete","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A turn of work, job."],"tags":["countable","obsolete","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A trick; deception."],"tags":["countable","obsolete","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/bɹeɪd/"}],"upstream_line":29338,"upstream_offset":194893120,"upstream_record_sha256":"e39d2557a81420d7222834f57dbe05681d9accfde72970ad8f32debddb8586d3","word":"braid"}
